                                                                       首程控股有限公司 2024年年報   3
GROUP OVERVIEW
集團簡介




      Shoucheng Holdings Limited (the “Company”/“Shoucheng Holdings”) relies
      on two core capabilities of FIME, which refers to fundraising, investment,
      management and exit and asset operation accumulated by the Company over the
      years, actively deploying digital-intelligent assets with long-term value, striving
      to become the leading service provider of China’s Intelligent Infrastructure Assets
      (“CIIA”).

      首程控股有限公司（「本公司」╱「首程控股」）融合公司多年來積累的資產融通與資
      產運營優勢，積極佈局具有長期價值的數智化資產，致力於成為領先的中國智能基礎
      設施資產服務商。

of financial markets and seeks to minimise potential adverse        法 減 低 對 本 集 團 財 務 表 現 的 潛 在 不 利 影 響。

With the vision of becoming the leading service provider of        本集團以致力於成為領先的中國智能基礎設

digital intelligence” model and established a closed-loop          集 團 著 重 通 過 收 益、利 潤、成 本、資 金、投 資
service for the full lifecycle management of infrastructure        回 報 以 及 融 資 活 動 之 嚴 格 管 理，以 取 得 經 常
assets. To achieve this goal, the Group focuses on disciplined     性 及 可 持 續 之 盈 利、現 金 流 及 股 息 分 派 而 不
management of revenue, profit, cost, capital, investment           影響本集團之財政實力及穩健性。
returns and financing activities, achieving recurring and
sustainable earnings, cash flow and dividend payment without
affecting the Group’s financial strength and stability.

The PRC laws and regulations require companies registered in the People’s         中國法律法規要求在中華人民共和國（「中國」，就本報
Republic of China (the “PRC”, for the purpose of this report shall exclude        告而言，不包括中國香港特別行政區（「香港」），中國澳
Hong Kong Special Administrative Region of the PRC (“Hong Kong”), the             門 特 別 行 政 區 及 中 國 台 灣）註 冊 之 公 司 從 各 自 法 定 財
Macao Special Administrative Region of the PRC and Taiwan, China) to              務 報 表 所 呈 報 的 淨 溢 利（抵 銷 以 往 年 度 之 累 計 虧 損 後）
provide for certain statutory reserves, which are to be appropriated from the     中，於分派溢利予權益持有人前，劃撥款項以提撥若干
net profit (after offsetting accumulated losses from prior years) as reported     法定儲備金。
in their respective statutory financial statements, before profit distributions
to equity holder.


All statutory reserves are created for specific purposes. The PRC company         所 有 法 定 儲 備 金 均 為 特 別 用 途 而 設 立。中 國 公 司 於 分
is required to appropriate 10% of net profits to statutory reserves, upon         派 當 年 度 之 除 稅 後 溢 利 時，須 向 法 定 儲 備 劃 撥 淨 溢 利
distribution of its post-tax profits of the current year. A company may           之10%。當 公 司 法 定 盈 餘 儲 備 的 總 和 超 過 其 註 冊 資 本
discontinue the contribution when the aggregate sum of the statutory              之50%時，公司可停止劃撥。法定儲備將僅可用作彌補
surplus reserve is more than 50% of its registered capital. The statutory         公司之虧損、擴大公司的生產營運或增加公司之資本。
reserves shall only be used to make up losses of the company, to expand the
company’s production operations, or to increase the capital of the company.

2   BASIS OF PREPARATION AND CHANGES IN                                                        2   編製基準及會計政策變動

          financial risks: market risk (including currency                                 市 場 風 險（包 括 貨 幣 風 險、利 率

          risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall                                     流動資金風險。本集團整體風險
          risk management programme focuses on the                                         管理計劃著重於金融市場的不
          unpredictability of financial markets and seeks to                               可預測性，並尋求方法減低對本
          minimise potential adverse effects on the Group’s                                集團財務表現的潛在不利影響。
          financial performance. The Company manages its                                   本公司根據其董事會制定的指
          financial risks in accordance with guidelines laid                               引管理其財務風險。經營財務部
          down by its Board of Directors. The Operating                                    與本集團內部密切合作確認並

          Group to cope with overall risk management,                                      險（包 括 貨 幣 風 險、利 率 風 險 及
          as well as specific areas, such as market risk                                   價 格 風 險）、信 貸 風 險 及 流 動 資

             Credit risk arises from deposits with banks                                   信貸風險來自銀行及其他金融
             and financial institutions, deposits and other                                機構存款、按金及其他應收款項
             receivables, as well as credit exposures to trading                           以及貿易客戶之信貸風險（包括
             customers, including outstanding receivables.                                 未償還應收款項）
                                                                                                  。


      (i)    Risk management                                                         (i)   風險管理

             I n o rd e r t o m i n i m i s e t h e c re d i t r i s k , t h e             為將信貸風險減至最低，本集團
             management of the Group has delegated a team                                  管理層已委派一組人員負責制
             responsible for determination of credit limits,                               訂信貸限額、信貸審批及其他監
             credit approvals and other monitoring procedures                              控程式，確保已採取跟進措施收
             to ensure that follow-up action is taken to recover                           回 逾 期 未 付 之 債 項。此 外，於 報
             overdue debts. In addition, the Group reviews the                             告期結束時，本集團會審閱各項
             recoverable amount of each individual trade debt                              個別貿易債項及其他應收款項
             and other receivable at the end of the reporting                              之可收回金額，確保已就不可收
             period to ensure that adequate impairment loss                                回金額計提合適之減值虧損。因
             is made for irrecoverable amounts. In this regard,                            此，董 事 們 認 為，本 集 團 之 信 貸
             the directors consider that the Group’s credit                                風 險 會 大 幅 減 少。此 外，本 集 團
             risk is significantly reduced. Besides, the Group                             要求客戶於服務前支付按金或
             has reduced credit risk on trade receivables by                               於交付前結償，以減低應收賬款

          Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments                                       第一級級別：於活躍市場買賣之

          that are not traded in an active market (for                                           之 金 融 工 具（如 場 外 衍 生 工 具）

          estimates. If all significant inputs required to                                       公允價值計量之工具之所有重

4   CRITICAL ESTIMATES AND JUDGEMENTS                        4   重大估計及判斷
    The preparation of consolidated financial statements         編 製 綜 合 財 務 報 表 須 使 用 會 計 估 計，
    requires the use of accounting estimates which,              按 照 定 義 將 很 少 等 於 實 際 結 果。管 理
    by definition, will seldom equal the actual results.         層於應用本集團會計政策時亦需行使
    Management also needs to exercise judgement in               判斷。
    applying the Group’s accounting policies.


    Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated.          估 計 及 判 斷 會 不 斷 評 估。彼 等 乃 基 於
    They are based on historical experience and other            過 往 經 驗 及 其 他 因 素，包 括 對 根 據 情
    factors, including expectations of future events that        況 認 為 可 能 對 實 體 造 成 財 務 影 響，且
    may have a financial impact on the entity and that are       屬合理的未來事件的預期。
    believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.


    4.1   Critical accounting estimates                          4.1   關鍵會計估計
    (a)   Estimation of the fair values of investment            (a)   投資物業公允價值估計
          properties
          Information about the valuation of investment                投資物業之估值資料已列於附
          properties is provided in Note 14.                           註14。


    (b)   Estimation of goodwill impairment                      (b)   商譽減值估計
          The Group tests whether goodwill has suffered                本集團每年將測試一次商譽減
          any impairment on an annual basis. For the 2024              值 估 計。於2024和2023報 告 期
          and 2023 reporting periods, the recoverable                  內，現金產生單元之可收回金額
          amount of cash-generating units (CGUs) was                   乃根據使用價值計算釐定。使用
          determined based on value-in-use calculations                價值計算採用基於管理層批准
          which require the use of assumptions. The                    之五年期財務預算及貼現率之
          calculations use cash flow projections based                 現金流量預測。
          on financial budgets approved by management
          covering a five-year period.


          Cash flows beyond the five-year period are                   超過五年期之現金流量採用附
          extrapolated using the estimated growth rates                註15列示的估計增長率推算。該
          stated in Note 15. These growth rates are                    等增長率與行業報告預測一致。
          consistent with forecasts included in industry
          reports.


          Details of impairment charge, key assumptions                減值、關鍵假設和可能改變之關

13(B) LEASES (continued)                                            13(B) 租賃（續）
     (iii)   The Group’s leasing activities and how                     (iii)   本集團之租賃活動及其入賬
             these are accounted for                                            方式
             The Group leases various office premises and                       本集團租用多項寫字樓物業及
             car parking assets. Lease contracts are typically                  停車資產。租賃合約通常按為期
             made for periods of 1 to 20 years, but may have                    1至20年訂立，惟如下文第(iv)項
             extension options as described in (iv) below.                      所述可選擇予以延期。租賃條款
             Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis                  乃按個別基準協商且包含多種
             and contain a wide range of different terms and                    不同條款及條件。租賃協議並無
             conditions. The lease agreements do not impose                     施加任何契諾。
             any covenants.


             Contracts may contain both lease and non-                          合約可包含租賃及非租賃部分。
             lease components. The Group allocates the                          本集團根據其相對獨立的價格
             consideration in the contract to the lease and                     將合約的代價分配至租賃及非
             non-lease components based on their relative                       租 賃 部 分。然 而，就 本 集 團 作 為
             stand-alone prices. However, for leases of real                    承租人租賃房地產而言，其已選
             estate for which the Group is a lessee, it has                     擇不區分租賃及非租賃部分，而
             elected not to separate lease and non-lease                        是將該等租賃入賬作為單一租
             components and instead accounts for these as a                     賃部分。
             single lease component.


             Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis                  租賃條款乃在個別基礎上協商，
             and contain a wide range of different terms and                    並包含各種不同的條款及條件。
             conditions. The lease agreements do not impose                     除了出租人持有之租賃資產擔
             any covenants other than the security interests                    保權益，租賃協議並無施加任何
             in the leases assets that are held by the lessor.                  契諾。租賃資產不得用作借款的
             Leased assets may not be used as security for                      抵押品。
             borrowing purposes.


             The lease payments are discounted using the                        租賃付款採用租賃中隱含的利
             interest rate implicit in the lease. If that rate                  率進行貼現。倘無法確定該利率
             cannot be readily determined, which is generally                   （本 集 團 租 賃 通 常 如 此），則 採
             the case for leases in the Group, the lessee’s                     用承租人的增量借款利率，即個
             incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate                 別承租人為於類似經濟環境下
             that the individual lessee would have to pay to                    獲得與使用權資產價值相近的
             borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of                   資 產，以 類 似 條 款、擔 保 及 條 件
             similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar               借入所需資金而將須予支付的
             economic environment with similar terms, security                  利率。
             and conditions.

              To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the                     為釐定增量借款利率，本集團：
              Group:


              •        where possible, uses recent third-party                     •   於可能情況下，以個別 承
                       financing received by the individual lessee                     租人近期取得的第三方
                       as a starting point, adjusted to reflect                        融資作為起點，作出調 整
                       changes in financing conditions since third                     以反映自接獲第三方融
                       party financing was received;                                   資以來融資情況的變化；


              •        uses a build-up approach that starts with                   •   使用累加法，首先就本 集
                       a risk-free interest rate adjusted for credit                   團所持有租賃的信貸風險
                       risk for leases held by the Group, which                        （近 期 並 無 第 三 方 融 資）
                       does not have recent third party financing;                     調整無風險利率；及
                       and


              •        makes adjustments specific to the lease,                    •   對租賃進行特定的調整，
                       e.g. term, country, currency and security.                      例 如 期 限、國 家、貨 幣 及
                                                                                       擔保。


              If a readily observable amortising loan rate is                      如果個別承租人（透過最近的融
              available to the individual lessee (through recent                   資或市場數據）可以獲得易於觀
              financing or market data) which has a similar                        察的攤銷貸款利率，且該貸款具
              payment profile to the lease, then the group                         有與租賃類似的付款情況，則集
              entities use that rate as a starting point to                        團企業將使用該利率作為確定
              determine the incremental borrowing rate.                            增量借款利率的起點。


              The Group is exposed to potential future increases                   本集團未來可能增加基於指數
              in variable lease payments based on an index or                      或利率的可變租賃付款，而該等
              rate, which are not included in the lease liability                  可變租賃付款在生效前不會計
              until they take effect. When adjustments to lease                    入租賃負債。當對基於指數或利

              the lease liability is reassessed and adjusted                       時，租賃負債會就使用權資產進
              against the right-of-use asset.                                      行重新評估及調整。

14   INVESTMENT PROPERTIES (continued)                           14   投資物業（續）
     Valuation techniques                                             估值技術
     The Group obtains valuations for its investment                  本集團至少每年一次就其投資物業取
     properties at least annually.                                    得估值。


     At the end of each reporting period, the directors               於 各 報 告 期 末，董 事 均 經 考 慮 最 近 期
     update their assessment of the fair value of each                估值更新彼等就各物業的公允價值的
     property, taking into account the most recent valuations.        評 估。董 事 於 合 理 公 允 價 值 估 計 範 疇
     The directors determine a property’s value within a range        內釐定物業價值。
     of reasonable fair value estimates.


     The best evidence of fair value is current prices in             公允價值的最佳憑證為相類似的物業
     an active market for similar properties. Where such              在 活 躍 市 場 的 當 時 價 格。如 未 能 取 得
     information is not available the directors consider              有 關 資 料，董 事 則 會 考 慮 來 自 多 方 面
     information from a variety of sources including:                 的資料，包括：


     •      current prices in an active market for properties         •    不同性質的物業於活躍市場的
            of different nature or recent prices of similar                當時價格或類似物業在比較不
            properties in less active markets, adjusted to                 活躍市場中的近期價格（須就反
            reflect those differences                                      映上述差異作出調整）


     •      discounted cash flow projections based on reliable        •    根據未來現金流量的可靠估計
            estimates of future cash flows                                 而預測的折算現金流量


     •      capitalised income projections based upon a               •    根據物業估計市場收入淨額以
            property’s estimated net market income, and a                  及源自市場證據分析的資本化
            capitalisation rate derived from an analysis of                比率而預測的資本化收入。
            market evidence.


     There were no changes to the valuation techniques                本年度內估值技術未發生變動。
     during the year.

      (b)     Goodwill impairment assessment                                       (b)   商譽減值評估


              For the purpose of impairment test of goodwill, goodwill                   商譽會被分配至現金產生單元以進行
              is allocated to groups of CGU(s). Such groups of CGU(s)                    減 值 測 試。此 等 現 金 產 生 單 位 乃 就 內
              represent the lowest level within the Group for which the                  部管理目的而用於監察商譽的最小單
              goodwill is monitored for internal management purpose.                     元。截 至2024年 及2023年12月31日 止
              The Group has only one group of CGU(s) according to its                    年度，根據業務運營情況，本集團只有
              business operation during the year ended 31 December                       一 個 現 金 產 生 單 位。於2024年 及2023
              2024 and 2023. Impairment review on the goodwill of                        年12月31日，管 理 層 已 對 本 集 團 商 譽
              the Group has been conducted by the management as at                       進 行 了 減 值 複 核。本 集 團 對 現 金 產 生
              31 December 2024 and 2023. The Group conducted the                         單 位 之 商 譽 的 年 度 減 值 測 試，通 過 將
              annual impairment test on goodwill for its group of CGU(s)                 其可回收金額與截至2024年及2023年
              by comparing its recoverable amount to its carrying amount                 12月31日 止 之 賬 面 金 額 進 行 比 較。可
              as at 31 December 2024 and 2023. The recoverable amount                    收回金額以資產之公允價值扣除出售

      Trade receivables are amounts due from customers for           應收賬款乃指就本集團於正常業務過
      goods sold or services performed in the ordinary course        程中所售商品或所提供之服務應收客
      of business. Trade receivables are recognised initially        戶 之 款 項。應 收 賬 款 初 步 按 無 條 件 代
      at the amount of consideration that is unconditional           價 金 額 確 認，除 非 其 包 含 重 大 融 資 成
      unless they contain significant financing components,          分，則 按 公 允 價 值 確 認。本 集 團 持 有 應
      when they are recognised at fair value. The Group holds        收 賬 款，目 的 為 收 取 合 約 現 金 流 量，因
      the trade receivables with the objective to collect the        此 採 用 實 際 利 率 法 按 攤 餘 成 本 計 量。
      contractual cash flows and therefore measures them             有 關 本 集 團 減 值 政 策 的 說 明，請 參 閱
      subsequently at amortised cost using the effective             附註36.9(d)。
      interest method. See Note 36.9(d) for a description of
      the Group’s impairment policies.


      Due to the short-term nature of the trade receivables,         由 於 應 收 賬 款 屬 於 短 期 性 質，其 賬 面
      their carrying amounts are considered to be the same as        值被視為與其公允價值相同。
      their fair value.


      The Group applies the HKFRS 9 simplified approach to           本集團應用香港財務報告準則第9號簡
      measure expected credit losses which uses a lifetime           化 方 法 計 量 預 期 信 貸 虧 損，為 所 有 應
      expected loss allowance for all trade receivables.             收 賬 款 使 用 終 生 預 期 虧 損 撥 備。應 收
      Movement in the provision for impairment losses for            賬款之減值虧損撥備之變動載列如下：

     36.3 Separate financial statements                             36.3 獨立財務報表
          Investments in subsidiaries are accounted for                於附屬公司之投資已按成本減
          at cost less impairment. Cost includes direct                減值列賬。成本包括投資之直接
          attributable costs of investment. The results of             應佔成本。附屬公司之業績已由
          subsidiaries are accounted for by the Company on             本公司按已收及應收股息列賬。
          the basis of dividend received and receivable.


          Impairment testing of the investments in                     從 該 等 投 資 收 取 股 息 時，如 股
          subsidiaries is required upon receiving a dividend           息超過附屬公司於宣派股息期
          from these investments if the dividend exceeds               內 之 全 面 收 益 總 額，或 該 等 投
          the total comprehensive income of the subsidiary             資於獨立財務報表內之賬面金
          in the period the dividend is declared or if the             額超過所投資公司淨資產（包括
          carrying amount of the investment in the separate            商譽）於綜合財務報表之賬面金
          financial statements exceeds the carrying amount             額，則於附屬公司之投資須進行
          in the consolidated financial statements of the              減值測試。
          investee’s net assets including goodwill.

          chief operating decision makers that are used to             釐定經營分部。於達成本集團之
          make strategic decisions and resources allocation.           呈報分部時，並無加總經主要營
          No operating segments identified by the chief                運決策人識別之各營運分部。
          operating decision makers have been aggregated
          in arriving at the reportable segments of the
          Group.

          might be impaired. Other assets are tested for                或情況有變顯示賬面金額可能

          For the purposes of assessing impairment, assets              流量（其於較大程度上獨立於自

          other assets or groups of assets (cash-generating             有減值，於各報告期末就能撥回

      36.19 Provisions                                                    36.19 撥備
             Provisions for legal claims, service warranties                  倘本集團須就過去事項承擔現
             and make good obligations are recognised when                    有法律或推定責任、履行該責任
             the Group has a present legal or constructive                    可能導致資源流出、及有關金額
             obligation as a result of past events, it is probable            能 可 靠 估 計，方 就 法 律 索 償、服
             that an outflow of resources will be required                    務權證及履行義務確認撥備。概
             to settle the obligation and the amount can be                   不就未來營運虧損確認撥備。
             reliably estimated. Provisions are not recognised
             for future operating losses.


             Where there are a number of similar obligations                  倘承擔若干類似責任，於釐定解
             as a whole. A provision is recognised even if the                除責任是否導致資源流出時，將
             likelihood of an outflow with respect to any one                 以整類責任類別為考慮。即使同
             item included in the same class of obligations may               類責任中任何一項導致資源流
             be small.                                                        出可能性甚低，亦會確認撥備。


             Provisions are measured at the present value of                  撥備按管理層對報告期末履行
             management’s best estimate of the expenditure                    現有責任所需支出之最佳估計
             required to settle the present obligation at the                 之現值計量。用於釐定現值之貼
             end of the reporting period. The discount rate                   現率為反映現時市場對金錢時
             used to determine the present value is a pre-tax                 間值及負債特定風險之評估之
             rate that reflects current market assessments of                 稅前利率。隨著時間流逝而增加
             the time value of money and the risks specific                   之撥備確認為利息開支。
             to the liability. The increase in the provision due
             to the passage of time is recognised as interest
             expense.

                 of the Company, excluding any costs                              包括普通股以外之任何
                 of servicing equity other than ordinary                          權 益 成 本）除 以 於 財 政 年
                 shares by the weighted average number                            度內已發行普通股之加
                 of ordinary shares outstanding during the                        權平均數，並就年內發行
                 financial year, adjusted for bonus elements                      之普通股之紅利元素作
                 in ordinary shares issued during the year                        出調整且不包括庫存股
                 and excluding treasury shares.                                   份計算。


          (b)   Diluted earnings per share                               (b)     每股稀釋盈利
                 Diluted earnings per share adjusts the                           每股稀釋盈利調整用於
                 figures used in the determination of basic                       釐定每股基本盈利的數
                 earnings per share to take into account                          字，以計及與潛在稀釋普
                 the after income tax effect of interest                          通股有關之利息及其他
                 and other financing costs associated with                        融資成本之除所得稅影
                 dilutive potential ordinary shares, and the                      響，及假設所有潛在稀釋
                 weighted average number of additional                            普通股獲轉換，則已發行
                 ordinary shares that would have been                             之普通股加權平均數將
                 outstanding assuming the conversion of all                       會增加。
                 dilutive potential ordinary shares.


     36.21 Dividend income                                            36.21 股息收入
          Dividends are received from financial assets                    股息來自以按公允價值計入損
          measured at FVPL and at FVOCI. Dividends are                    益及按公允價值計入其他全面
          recognised as other income in profit or loss when               收益計量之金融資產。股息於收
          the right to receive payment is established. This               取付款之權利確立時於損益內
          applies even if they are paid out of pre-acquisition            確認為其他收益。即使股息自收
          profits, unless the dividend clearly represents a               購 前 溢 利 撥 付，此 亦 適 用，除 非
          recovery of part of the cost of an investment.                  股息明確代表收回部分投資成
          In this case, the dividend is recognised in OCI if              本。於 該 情 況 下，倘 股 息 與 以 按
          it relates to an investment measured at FVOCI.                  公允價值計入其他全面收益計
          However, the investment may need to be tested                   量之投資有關，則於其他全面收
          for impairment as a consequence.                                益 內 確 認。然 而，投 資 因 而 可 能
                                                                          需要進行減值測試。

      36.22 Leases                                                       36.22 租賃
             Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and               在租賃資產在本集團的起始使
             a corresponding liability at the date at which the              用日，租賃會確認為使用權資產
             leased asset is available for use by the Group.                 及相應租賃負債。

             Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are                 租賃產生的資產及負債初步以
             initially measured on a present value basis. Lease              現值進行計量。租賃負債包括以
             liabilities include the net present value of the                下租賃付款的淨現值：
             following lease payments:


             •       fixed payments (including in-substance                  •      固 定 付 款（包 括 實 質 固 定
                     fixed payments), less any lease incentives                     付 款），減 任 何 應 收 租 賃
                     receivable;                                                    優惠；

             •       variable lease payment that are based on                •      基於指數或利率的可變
                     an index or a rate, initially measured using                   租賃付款，初步以開始 日
                     the index or rate as at the commencement                       期的指數或利率計量；
                     date;


             •       amounts expected to be payable by the                   •      剩餘價值擔保下本集團
                     Group under residual value guarantees;                         預期應付款項；

             •       the exercise price of a purchase option if              •      購 買 選 擇 權 的 行 使 價（倘
                     the Group is reasonably certain to exercise                    本集團合理確定行使該
                     that option; and                                               選擇權）  ；及

             •       payments of penalties for terminating the               •      支 付 終 止 租 賃 的 罰 款（倘
                     lease, if the lease term reflects the Group                    租賃條款反映本集團行
                     exercising that option.                                        使權利終止租賃）      。

             Lease payments to be made under reasonably                      根據合理確定續租選擇權將予
             certain extension options are also included in the              作出的租賃付款亦納入負債計
             measurement of the liability.                                   量。

             Lease payments are allocated between principal                  租賃付款於本金及融資成本之
             and finance cost. The finance cost is charged                   間作出分配。融資成本於租期內
             to profit or loss over the lease period so as to                自損益支銷，藉以令各期間的負
             produce a constant periodic rate of interest on                 債餘額的期間利率一致。
             the remaining balance of the liability for each
             period.

      36.25 Interest income                                             36.25 利息收入
             Interest income from financial assets at FVPL                  按公允價值計入損益之金融資
             is included in the net fair value gains on these               產所產生之利息收入於損益確
             assets, see Note 7.                                            認為公允價值淨收益，請參閱附
                                                                            註7。


             Interest income on financial assets at amortised               按攤餘成本入賬之金融資產採
             cost calculated using the effective interest                   用實際利率法計算的利息收入
             method is recognised in profit or loss as part of              於損益確認為其他收入的部分。
             other income.


             Interest income is presented as other income                   由現金管理之金融資產所產生
             where it is earned from financial assets that are              之利息收入將於其他收入確認
             held for cash management purposes, see Note 6.                 為利息收入，請參閱附註6。


             Interest income is calculated by applying the                  金融資產之利息收入之計算為
             effective interest rate to the gross carrying                  採用實際年利率除以金融資產
             amount of a financial asset except for financial               之賬面總值，除隨後信貸虧損之
             assets that subsequently become credit impaired.               金融資產外。已信貸虧損之金融
             For credit-impaired financial assets the effective             資產採用實際年利率除以金融
             interest rate is applied to the net carrying amount            資產之賬面淨值。
             of the financial asset (after deduction of the loss
             allowance).
